```bash
echo '=== DETERMINISM base vs base2 (same composer twice)'; python3 -B -u $M/noop_compare.py $M/c21/base $M/c21/base2 | head -1
echo '=== BYTES tip vs ctlshift (0.01 pt shift)'; python3 -B -u $M/noop_compare.py $M/c21/tip $M/c21/ctlshift | head -1
echo '=== TRACES pre21 vs base (pre-㉑ composer)'; python3 -B -u $M/noop_compare.py $M/c21/pre21 $M/c21/base | grep -aE 'record\(s\) changed|svg DIFFERS|^TOTALS'
```

Expected (observed):

```
=== DETERMINISM base vs base2 (same composer twice)
CNX_Chem_14_03_corresp: laid-out 54  svg identical  png identical  trace equal  report added [] removed [] changed []
=== BYTES tip vs ctlshift (0.01 pt shift)
CNX_Chem_14_03_corresp: laid-out 54  svg DIFFERS  png DIFFERS  trace equal  report added [] removed [] changed []
=== TRACES pre21 vs base (pre-㉑ composer)
CNX_Chem_14_01_conjugate_img: laid-out 12  svg DIFFERS  png DIFFERS  trace 1 record(s) changed ['NH4|+ (conjugate acid)']  report added [] removed [] changed []
CNX_Chem_18_07_Nitrogen: laid-out 11  svg DIFFERS  png DIFFERS  trace 3 record(s) changed ['ammonium (NH4|+|)', 'nitrites (NO2|–', 'nitrates (NO3|–']  report added [] removed [] changed []
TOTALS 27 figure(s), 163 laid-out decide record(s) in A: svg differ 2, png differ 2, trace records changed 4, key sequences differ 0; report keys added {} removed [] changed []
```

How to read the three controls:
- **Determinism.** The same composer twice is byte-identical, so a byte comparison means something.
- **Bytes.** A 0.01 pt shift moves the SVG and the PNG while the trace stays equal, so `svg identical` is a
  measurement and not a blind spot.
- **Traces.** The pre-㉑ composer differs from the base in exactly ㉑'s registered prediction: 4 records in 2
  figures (Nitrogen ×3, conjugate_img ×1) and 2 SVGs. So the trace comparison sees a real decide change.

(The `VERDICT DIFFERENCES` lines that a full control run prints are expected: the verdict encodes the no-op's
prediction, not a control's.)

- [ ] **Step 7: Build the A/B's two scratch configs FROM the value sheet (no value retyped)**

Create `$M/make_ab_configs.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§C140 ㊾ D5(a), Part 5 Task 11: build the two SCRATCH configs of the 8-directory A/B (design D-j), 0 ISK.

    python3 -B -u make_ab_configs.py WT OUT_DIR

Reads [USER]'s values out of WT/docs/handoffs/2026-10-03-step2-value-sheet.md (rows A1-A3 and B1-B6, the
"Value from [USER]" column) and applies the design's "PR-B handoff: transcription rules" BY VALUE, so no value
is retyped: keys are the first backtick span of the key cell with the Markdown `\\|` made a bare `|`; a value
cell first drops its trailing `*( ... )*` chat note (A1, B1); a value cell then made of backtick spans (B4-B6) is those
spans joined with a newline, after the ` / ` separators and a trailing ` stacked` are removed. Writes
OUT_DIR/scratch-values.json (the committed config with heldBlockValues = those values) and
OUT_DIR/scratch-empty.json (the same config with heldBlockValues = {}). Every value is checked by the
committed heldvalues.parse_value. It prints keys, line counts and checks, never a value.
"""
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
OUT = Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
FTT = WT / 'experiments' / 'figure-text-translation'
sys.path.insert(0, str(FTT))
import heldvalues  # noqa: E402  (stdlib only: no _deps, no pylibs)

SHEET = WT / 'docs' / 'handoffs' / '2026-10-03-step2-value-sheet.md'
ROWS = ['A1', 'A2', 'A3', 'B1', 'B2', 'B3', 'B4', 'B5', 'B6']
fails = []


def check(label, ok, detail=''):
    print(f"  {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}  {label}{': ' + detail if detail else ''}", flush=True)
    if not ok:
        fails.append(label)


def spans(cell):
    return re.findall(r'`([^`]*)`', cell)


table = {}
order = []
for line in SHEET.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines():
    m = re.match(r'^\| (A\d|B\d) \|', line)
    if not m:
        continue
    cells = [c.strip() for c in re.split(r'(?<!\\)\|', line)[1:-1]]
    row, fig_cell, key_cell, value_cell = cells[0], cells[1], cells[2], cells[-1]
    basename = spans(fig_cell)[0]
    key = spans(key_cell)[0].replace('\\|', '|')
    # The chat note goes FIRST: B1's note itself quotes the superseded value (the one without the
    # formula) in backticks, so a span rule applied before it would draw exactly that value.
    value_cell = re.sub(r'\s*\*\(.*\)\*\s*$', '', value_cell).strip()
    if spans(value_cell):
        rest = re.sub(r'`[^`]*`', '', value_cell).replace(' stacked', '')
        check(f'{row} a span-built value has only ` / ` between its spans', set(rest) <= set(' /'), repr(rest))
        value = '\n'.join(spans(value_cell))
    else:
        value = value_cell
    check(f'{row} value carries no Markdown (* ` \\ |)', not set(value) & set('*`\\|'))
    table.setdefault(basename, {})[key] = value
    order.append((row, basename, key, value))

check('the sheet yields exactly rows A1-A3 and B1-B6, in order', [r for r, *_ in order] == ROWS,
      repr([r for r, *_ in order]))
check('9 keys over 7 basenames', sum(map(len, table.values())) == 9 and len(table) == 7,
      f'{sum(map(len, table.values()))} keys, {len(table)} basenames')
want_lines = {'B4': 3, 'B5': 3, 'B6': 2}
for row, basename, key, value in order:
    lines = heldvalues.parse_value(value)            # raises HeldValueError on any malformed value
    decoded = [''.join(ch for ch, _ in ln) for ln in lines]
    n = want_lines.get(row, 1)
    check(f'{row} {basename} {key!r}: {len(lines)} line(s), parse_value accepts it', len(lines) == n,
          f'want {n}')
    if row in ('B4', 'B5'):
        k = key.split('|')
        check(f'{row} lines 0 and 2 decode to the key\'s own lines 0 and 2 (unchanged, by value)',
              decoded[0] == k[0] and decoded[2] == k[2] and decoded[1] != k[1])
    if row == 'B6':
        check('B6 line 0 decodes to the key\'s line 0, `C` (unchanged); line 1 differs',
              decoded[0] == key.split('|')[0] == 'C' and decoded[1] != key.split('|')[1])
    if row == 'B1':
        check('B1 begins with the LETTER O (U+004F), then a lowered 2', value[:2] == 'O₂')
    if row in ('A1', 'A2', 'A3', 'B2', 'B3'):
        check(f'{row} is one line and differs from its key', '\n' not in value and decoded[0] != key)

cfg = json.loads((FTT / 'figure-text.config.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
check('the committed config ships heldBlockValues EMPTY', cfg.get('heldBlockValues') == {},
      repr(cfg.get('heldBlockValues')))
OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
for name, held in (('scratch-values.json', table), ('scratch-empty.json', {})):
    c = dict(cfg)
    c['heldBlockValues'] = held
    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(c, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    back = heldvalues.load_table(json.loads((OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8')))
    check(f'{name} round-trips through heldvalues.load_table', back == held, f'{len(back)} figure(s)')
for basename in sorted(table):
    print(f"  {basename}: {[(k, v.count(chr(10)) + 1) for k, v in table[basename].items()]}", flush=True)
print(f"AB-CONFIGS {'OK' if not fails else 'FAILED: ' + ', '.join(fails)}: "
      f"{sum(map(len, table.values()))} keys over {len(table)} basenames -> {OUT}", flush=True)
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)
```

```bash
python3 -B -u $M/make_ab_configs.py "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" $M/ab
```

Expected (observed). It prints keys and line counts, never a value:

```

  PASS  A2 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  A3 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  B1 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  B2 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  B3 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  B4 a span-built value has only ` / ` between its spans: ' /  / '
  PASS  B4 value carries no Markdown (* ` \ |)
  PASS  B5 a span-built value has only ` / ` between its spans: ' /  / '

  PASS  9 keys over 7 basenames: 9 keys, 7 basenames
  PASS  A1 CNX_Chem_01_02_MattType 'No': 1 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 1

  PASS  B1 CNX_Chem_09_05_MolSpeed1 '02 at T = 300 K': 1 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 1
  PASS  B1 begins with the LETTER O (U+004F), then a lowered 2
  PASS  B2 CNX_Chem_14_02_phscale '100 or 1': 1 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 1
  PASS  B2 is one line and differs from its key
  PASS  B3 CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer '[CH3CO2H] is 11% of [CH3CO2|–]': 1 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 1
  PASS  B3 is one line and differs from its key
  PASS  B4 CNX_Chem_18_04_OxStNonmts '4+|To|4–': 3 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 3
  PASS  B4 lines 0 and 2 decode to the key's own lines 0 and 2 (unchanged, by value)
  PASS  B5 CNX_Chem_18_04_OxStNonmts '5+|To|3–': 3 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 3
  PASS  B5 lines 0 and 2 decode to the key's own lines 0 and 2 (unchanged, by value)
  PASS  B6 CNX_Chem_20_04_amide1_img 'C|H or R': 2 line(s), parse_value accepts it: want 2
  PASS  B6 line 0 decodes to the key's line 0, `C` (unchanged); line 1 differs
  PASS  the committed config ships heldBlockValues EMPTY: {}
  PASS  scratch-values.json round-trips through heldvalues.load_table: 7 figure(s)
  PASS  scratch-empty.json round-trips through heldvalues.load_table: 0 figure(s)
  CNX_Chem_01_02_MattType: [('No', 1)]
  CNX_Chem_07_04_HNO2_img: [('or', 1)]
  CNX_Chem_09_05_MolSpeed1: [('02 at T = 300 K', 1)]
  CNX_Chem_14_02_phscale: [('100 or 1', 1)]
  CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer: [('[CH3CO2H] is 11% of [CH3CO2|–]', 1)]
  CNX_Chem_18_04_OxStNonmts: [('4+|To|4–', 3), ('5+|To|3–', 3)]
  CNX_Chem_20_04_amide1_img: [('R or H', 1), ('C|H or R', 2)]
AB-CONFIGS OK: 9 keys over 7 basenames -> /home/siggi/.cache/namsbokasafn-audit/2026-10-03-step2/p5-measure/ab
```

⚠️ **A transcription hazard that this script measured, and its order of operations now encodes.** B1's chat note
`*(corrected in chat … the sheet first read `…`, which would have dropped the formula …)*` itself contains a
backtick span: the SUPERSEDED value, without O₂. A first draft applied the span rule before stripping the note,
so B1 would have drawn exactly the value [USER] corrected away. Two of its own checks refused that draft:
`B1 a span-built value has only ` / ` between its spans` and `B1 begins with the LETTER O (U+004F), then a
lowered 2`. The note is now stripped first. PR-B's data commit transcribes the same cell, so its executor should
expect the same trap.

- [ ] **Step 8: The committed validator on the scratch config: design D-g's "all 7 PR-B figures pass", measured, with a control**

Create `$M/validate_scratch.mjs`:

```js
// §C140 ㊾ D5(a), Part 5 Task 11: run the COMMITTED config validator over a scratch config (0 ISK, read-only).
// usage: node validate_scratch.mjs <WT> <config.json>
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'url';

const [wt, cfgPath] = process.argv.slice(2);
const { validateFigureConfig, buildValidatorCorpus } = await import(
  pathToFileURL(path.join(wt, 'tools/lib/figure-config-validate.js')).href
);
const cfg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cfgPath, 'utf-8'));
const corpus = buildValidatorCorpus(wt, cfg);
const problems = validateFigureConfig(cfg, corpus);
for (const [b, st] of Object.entries(corpus.heldState || {})) {
  console.log(
    `  ${b}: svgRows ${st.svgRows} translatedCopies ${st.translatedCopies} sidecarKeys ${
      st.sidecarKeys === null ? 'null (no sidecar)' : st.sidecarKeys.length
    }`
  );
}
for (const p of problems) console.log(`  PROBLEM ${p}`);
console.log(
  `VALIDATOR ${problems.length === 0 ? 'OK' : 'REFUSED'}: ${problems.length} problem(s), ` +
    `${Object.keys(cfg.heldBlockValues || {}).length} heldBlockValues figure(s), ` +
    `${Object.keys(corpus.heldState || {}).length} in heldState`
);
process.exitCode = problems.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Run the A/B: three arms per figure, each on a FRESH copy, one process at a time**

Create `$M/ab_run.sh`:

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# §C140 ㊾ D5(a), Part 5 Task 11: the 8-directory A/B (design D-j), 0 ISK.
# usage: ab_run.sh WT M
#   WT  the worktree (its books/ sidecars and its pylibs are read; nothing in it is written)
#   M   the evidence dir: M/trees/{tip,base}/experiments/figure-text-translation (git archive exports),
#       M/ab/scratch-{values,empty}.json (make_ab_configs.py). Writes M/ab/{values,empty,base}/<basename>/.
# Three arms per figure, each on a FRESH copy of the read-only vs-prepare directory, one process at a time:
#   values  the tip's figure-compose.py --config scratch-values.json
#   empty   the tip's figure-compose.py --config scratch-empty.json
#   base    29d3b27d8's figure-compose.py (no --config: it has none)
# A sidecar figure takes its committed sidecar as --translations; a textless one {"blocks": {}}, the shape
# tools/figure-run.js's recomposeTextless hands it. Same SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH in every arm (fontTools stamps it
# into the embedded font subset), FIGTEXT_OUT unset.
set -u
WT=$1; M=$2
VS=/home/siggi/.cache/namsbokasafn-audit/2026-10-03-step2/vs-prepare
SC=$WT/books/efnafraedi-2e/figure-text
PY=$WT/experiments/figure-text-translation/pylibs
export SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=1700000000
echo '{"blocks": {}}' > $M/ab/empty-translations.json
for b in CNX_Chem_01_02_MattType CNX_Chem_01_04_MYdCmIn CNX_Chem_07_04_HNO2_img CNX_Chem_09_05_MolSpeed1 \
         CNX_Chem_14_02_phscale CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer CNX_Chem_18_04_OxStNonmts CNX_Chem_20_04_amide1_img; do
  tr=$SC/$b.is.json; [ -f "$tr" ] || tr=$M/ab/empty-translations.json
  for arm in values empty base; do
    d=$M/ab/$arm/$b; rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p $M/ab/$arm; cp -r $VS/$b "$d"
    case $arm in
      values) tree=$M/trees/tip;  extra=(--config $M/ab/scratch-values.json) ;;
      empty)  tree=$M/trees/tip;  extra=(--config $M/ab/scratch-empty.json) ;;
      base)   tree=$M/trees/base; extra=() ;;
    esac
    avail=$(free -h | awk 'NR==2 {print $7}')
    ( cd $tree/experiments/figure-text-translation && env -u FIGTEXT_OUT FIGTEXT_PYLIBS=$PY python3 -B -u \
        figure-compose.py --out "$d" --translations "$tr" "${extra[@]}" > "$d.stdout" 2>&1 ); rc=$?
    echo "$b $arm EXIT=$rc avail=$avail tr=$(basename $tr)"
  done
done
echo "AB-RUN DONE"
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Read the A/B against D-j's predicted table, then prove the reader can fail**

Create `$M/ab_compare.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§C140 ㊾ D5(a), Part 5 Task 11: read the 8-directory A/B (design D-j) against its predicted table, 0 ISK.

    FIGTEXT_PYLIBS=<WT>/experiments/figure-text-translation/pylibs python3 -B -u ab_compare.py WT M

Reads M/ab/{values,empty,base}/<basename>/ (ab_run.sh) and M/ab/scratch-values.json. Widths come from the
pinned face FILES with fontTools (figis.face_path), independent of compose.py's cairo measure, the instrument
test_compose_held.py uses. It prints English keys, positions, sizes and verdicts, never a held value: a drawn
line is compared BY VALUE with the decoded value line (heldvalues.parse_value). It writes nothing.

Per figure: `held` (key, block, changed) with multiplicity against D-j; heldErrors empty; values.missing +
held keys == empty.missing (multisets); the <text> elements only the values arm draws, grouped into lines by
pen adjacency, each line's text equal to its decoded value line and its extent and baseline within 0.01 pt of
the D-c table; each script segment's size and baseline shift; the <text> elements only the empty arm draws
(the English the held lines replace); no U+2070-209F character in any SVG; MYdCmIn values == empty, bytes;
empty arm == base arm, translated.svg and translated.png bytes, and the report and compose.json differing only
by the held fields. Terminal line: `AB VERDICT PREDICTION HELD` or `AB VERDICT PREDICTION FAILED`.
"""
import json
import re
import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

WT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
M = Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
FTT = WT / 'experiments' / 'figure-text-translation'
sys.path.insert(0, str(FTT))
import _deps  # noqa: E402,F401  (puts pylibs on the path; FIGTEXT_PYLIBS names it)
from fontTools.ttLib import TTFont  # noqa: E402
from figis import face_path  # noqa: E402
import heldvalues  # noqa: E402

# design D-j / D-c: per figure, in draw (block) order, (key, block, changed, [(x0, x1, baseline) per changed line])
PREDICTED = {
    'CNX_Chem_01_02_MattType': [('No', 11, [0], [(144.70, 158.71, 117.89)]),
                                ('No', 13, [0], [(45.77, 59.78, 66.38)]),
                                ('No', 15, [0], [(281.93, 295.94, 66.38)])],
    'CNX_Chem_01_04_MYdCmIn': [],
    'CNX_Chem_07_04_HNO2_img': [('or', 8, [0], [(61.24, 76.26, 8.45)])],
    'CNX_Chem_09_05_MolSpeed1': [('02 at T = 300 K', 13, [0], [(124.79, 191.46, 83.30)])],
    'CNX_Chem_14_02_phscale': [('100 or 1', 5, [0], [(24.36, 63.28, 341.66)]),
                               ('100 or 1', 87, [0], [(74.01, 112.94, 35.48)])],
    'CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer': [('[CH3CO2H] is 11% of [CH3CO2|–]', 23, [0], [(101.72, 234.21, 150.37)])],
    'CNX_Chem_18_04_OxStNonmts': [('4+|To|4–', 2, [1], [(163.83, 170.05, 59.51)]),
                                  ('5+|To|3–', 4, [1], [(241.83, 248.05, 59.51)])],
    'CNX_Chem_20_04_amide1_img': [('C|H or R', 0, [1], [(54.25, 87.26, 83.49)]),
                                  ('R or H', 1, [0], [(132.76, 165.78, 93.49)]),
                                  ('R or H', 2, [0], [(122.00, 155.02, 66.12)])],
}
# design D-j: (size, shift) of the script segments, in draw order, per figure
SCRIPTS = {
    'CNX_Chem_09_05_MolSpeed1': [('2', 7.0002, -1.9998)],
    'CNX_Chem_14_02_phscale': [('0', 6.9998, 4.0004), ('0', 6.9998, 4.0004)],
    'CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer': [('3', 7.0002, -2.9997), ('2', 7.0002, -2.9997), ('3', 7.0002, -2.9997),
                              ('2', 7.0002, -2.9997), ('–', 7.0002, 3.9996)],
}
HELD_FIELDS = {'held', 'heldErrors', 'heldValuesPath', 'heldConfigPath'}
TOL = 0.01
fails = []
_FACES = {}


def check(label, ok, detail=''):
    print(f"  {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}  {label}{': ' + detail if detail else ''}", flush=True)
    if not ok:
        fails.append(label)


def adv(text, size, bold, italic):
    k = (bool(bold), bool(italic))
    if k not in _FACES:
        f = TTFont(str(face_path(k)))
        _FACES[k] = (f.getBestCmap(), f['hmtx'], f['head'].unitsPerEm)
    cmap, hmtx, upm = _FACES[k]
    return sum(hmtx[cmap.get(ord(c), '.notdef')][0] for c in text) * size / upm


def elements(svg, page_h):
    out = []
    for m in re.finditer(r'<text ([^>]*)>([^<]*)</text>', svg):
        a = dict(re.findall(r'([\w:-]+)="([^"]*)"', m.group(1)))
        t = m.group(2).replace('&lt;', '<').replace('&gt;', '>').replace('&amp;', '&')
        out.append(dict(text=t, x=float(a['x']), y=page_h - float(a['y']), size=float(a['font-size']),
                        bold=a.get('font-weight') == '700', italic=a.get('font-style') == 'italic',
                        family=a.get('font-family'), layout='font-kerning:none' in a.get('style', ''),
                        raw=m.group(0)))
    return out


def only_in(a, b):
    """The elements of `a` whose raw <text> is not matched in `b` (multiset), in `a`'s document order."""
    left = Counter(e['raw'] for e in b)
    out = []
    for e in a:
        if left[e['raw']]:
            left[e['raw']] -= 1
        else:
            out.append(e)
    return out


def lines_of(els):
    """Group laid-out segments into drawn lines by pen adjacency (the layout advances the pen by each segment's
    linear advance) and a baseline gate against the line's FIRST segment, which is a body run (a changed line
    that opens styled is refused): a script is shifted at most 0.4445 x the body size. (Gating against the
    PREVIOUS segment splits a subscript followed by a superscript, 7.0 pt apart on buffer.)"""
    lines = []
    for e in els:
        if lines:
            p, f = lines[-1][-1], lines[-1][0]
            if (abs(e['x'] - (p['x'] + adv(p['text'], p['size'], p['bold'], p['italic']))) <= 0.05
                    and abs(e['y'] - f['y']) <= 0.5 * f['size'] + 0.05):
                lines[-1].append(e)
                continue
        lines.append([e])
    return lines


values_cfg = json.loads((M / 'ab' / 'scratch-values.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['heldBlockValues']
n_lines = n_blocks = 0
figs_held = 0
for b, pred in PREDICTED.items():
    print(f'{b}', flush=True)
    V, E, B = (M / 'ab' / arm / b for arm in ('values', 'empty', 'base'))
    page_h = json.loads((V / 'meta.json').read_text())['page'][1]
    rv = json.loads((V / 'compose-report.json').read_text())
    re_ = json.loads((E / 'compose-report.json').read_text())
    rb = json.loads((B / 'compose-report.json').read_text())
    held = [(h['key'], h['block'], h['changed']) for h in rv['held']]
    check('held (key, block, changed) in draw order WITH multiplicity, as D-j', held == [p[:3] for p in pred],
          repr(held))
    check('heldErrors is empty', rv['heldErrors'] == [], repr(rv['heldErrors']))
    check('values.missing + held keys == empty.missing (multisets)',
          Counter(rv['missing']) + Counter(k for k, _, _ in held) == Counter(re_['missing']),
          f"values missing {len(rv['missing'])}, held {len(held)}, empty missing {len(re_['missing'])}")
    check('the empty arm draws nothing held', re_['held'] == [] and re_['heldErrors'] == [])
    sv, se, sb = ((d / 'translated.svg').read_text(encoding='utf-8') for d in (V, E, B))
    for arm, s in (('values', sv), ('empty', se), ('base', sb)):
        n = sum(0x2070 <= ord(c) <= 0x209F for c in s)
        check(f'no U+2070-209F character in the {arm} translated.svg', n == 0, f'{n}')
    ev, ee = elements(sv, page_h), elements(se, page_h)
    new, gone = only_in(ev, ee), only_in(ee, ev)
    check('every <text> only the values arm draws is a laid-out FigIS segment',
          all(e['layout'] and e['family'] == 'FigIS' for e in new),
          repr([(e['family'], e['layout']) for e in new if not (e['layout'] and e['family'] == 'FigIS')]))
    drawn = lines_of(new)
    want = [ln for p in pred for ln in p[3]]
    vlines = []
    for key, _, changed, _ in pred:
        dec = [''.join(ch for ch, _ in ln) for ln in heldvalues.parse_value(values_cfg[b][key])]
        vlines += [dec[i] for i in changed]
    check(f'{len(want)} new drawn line(s), as D-j', len(drawn) == len(want), f'{len(drawn)}')
    n_lines += len(drawn)
    n_blocks += len(pred)
    figs_held += bool(pred)
    for i, (ln, w, txt) in enumerate(zip(drawn, want, vlines)):
        body = max(e['size'] for e in ln)
        base = [e['y'] for e in ln if e['size'] == body]
        x0, last = ln[0]['x'], ln[-1]
        x1 = last['x'] + adv(last['text'], last['size'], last['bold'], last['italic'])
        same_text = ''.join(e['text'] for e in ln) == txt
        pos = abs(x0 - w[0]) <= TOL and abs(x1 - w[1]) <= TOL and all(abs(y - w[2]) <= TOL for y in base)
        check(f'line {i}: drawn text == its decoded value line (by value); extent {x0:.2f}..{x1:.2f} @'
              f'{base[0]:.2f}, size {body:.4f}, bold {ln[0]["bold"]} vs D-c {w[0]:.2f}..{w[1]:.2f} @{w[2]:.2f} '
              f'(within {TOL})', same_text and pos)
    scripts = [(e['text'], e['size'], round(e['y'] - max((x['y'] for ln in drawn for x in ln
                                                         if x['size'] == max(z['size'] for z in ln) and e in ln),
                                                        default=e['y']), 4))
               for ln in drawn for e in ln if e['size'] < max(z['size'] for z in ln)]
    ws = SCRIPTS.get(b, [])
    ok_s = len(scripts) == len(ws) and all(t == wt and abs(s - wz) <= TOL and abs(d - wd) <= TOL
                                           for (t, s, d), (wt, wz, wd) in zip(scripts, ws))
    check(f'script segments (text, size, shift) as D-j: {[(t, round(s, 3), d) for t, s, d in scripts]}', ok_s)
    if b == 'CNX_Chem_14_06_buffer' and drawn:
        ln = drawn[0]
        dash = [e for e in ln if e['text'] == '–']
        subs = [e for e in ln if e['size'] < max(z['size'] for z in ln) and e['text'] != '–']
        check('buffer: the U+2013 charge is drawn AFTER the last subscript (D-h limit 5)', bool(dash and subs)
              and dash[0]['x'] >= subs[-1]['x'] + adv(subs[-1]['text'], subs[-1]['size'], False, False) - TOL)
    print(f"    the English the values arm no longer draws: {[e['text'] for e in gone]}", flush=True)
    if b == 'CNX_Chem_01_04_MYdCmIn':
        check('MYdCmIn: values translated.svg and .png BYTE-identical to the empty arm',
              (V / 'translated.svg').read_bytes() == (E / 'translated.svg').read_bytes()
              and (V / 'translated.png').read_bytes() == (E / 'translated.png').read_bytes())
    check('empty arm == base arm (29d3b27d8): translated.svg and translated.png bytes',
          (E / 'translated.svg').read_bytes() == (B / 'translated.svg').read_bytes()
          and (E / 'translated.png').read_bytes() == (B / 'translated.png').read_bytes())
    extra = sorted(k for k in re_ if k not in rb)
    differ = sorted(k for k in rb if k in re_ and rb[k] != re_[k]) + sorted(k for k in rb if k not in re_)
    check('empty-arm report == base report except the four ADDED held fields',
          set(extra) == HELD_FIELDS and not differ and re_['heldValuesPath'] and re_['heldConfigPath'],
          f'added {extra} differ {differ}')
    cv, cb = (json.loads((d / 'compose.json').read_text()) for d in (E, B))
    # outputPath names each arm's OWN directory, so it is compared with the arm segment normalised
    cv['outputPath'] = cv['outputPath'].replace(f'/ab/empty/{b}/', '/ab/ARM/' + b + '/')
    cb['outputPath'] = cb['outputPath'].replace(f'/ab/base/{b}/', '/ab/ARM/' + b + '/')
    check('empty-arm compose.json == base compose.json plus `held: []` (outputPath arm-normalised)',
          {k: v for k, v in cv.items() if k != 'held'} == cb and cv.get('held') == [],
          f"keys {sorted(set(cv) ^ set(cb))} outputPath {cv['outputPath'] == cb['outputPath']}")
    out_v, out_e = ((M / 'ab' / arm / f'{b}.stdout').read_text(encoding='utf-8') for arm in ('values', 'empty'))
    note = f'NOTE (not a failure): {len(pred)} label(s) drawn from heldBlockValues' in out_v
    check('stdout: the values arm prints the held NOTE (when it draws any) and never the refusal block, the '
          'empty arm neither', (note if pred else 'drawn from heldBlockValues' not in out_v)
          and 'heldBlockValues entr' not in out_v and 'drawn from heldBlockValues' not in out_e)
print(f'AB TOTALS {n_lines} changed line(s) in {n_blocks} block(s) of {figs_held} figure(s); '
      f'{len(fails)} failed check(s)', flush=True)
print(f"AB VERDICT {'PREDICTION HELD' if not fails and (n_lines, n_blocks, figs_held) == (13, 13, 7) else 'PREDICTION FAILED: ' + ', '.join(fails)}",
      flush=True)
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)
```

⚠️ **The reader's first draft had two INSTRUMENT defects, both found by reading raw elements before believing a
red.**
1. It gated each segment's baseline against the PREVIOUS segment. buffer's subscript `2` and its superscript
   `–` sit 7.0 pt apart (147.365 vs 154.365), so it split buffer's one line into two: `2 new drawn line(s)`.
   The raw SVG put the `–` at x 227.812, exactly at the `2`'s pen end. The gate now compares with the line's
   first segment.

   arm-normalised.

Neither defect was in the composer, and the A/B arms were not re-run.

A full run takes about 23 minutes, which is over the 10-minute foreground limit. Run each one ALONE, in the
background or in a terminal, with nothing else running, and read its exit code from a marker file, never from a
pipe. 29d3b27d8 needs its own worktree with both `node_modules` trees linked (pylibs and `sources.local.json`
too, so both ends see the same environment).

```bash

git worktree add --detach $B 29d3b27d8 && ln -s $MAIN/node_modules $B/node_modules && ln -s $MAIN/server/node_modules $B/server/node_modules && ln -s $MAIN/experiments/figure-text-translation/pylibs $B/experiments/figure-text-translation/pylibs && cp experiments/figure-text-translation/sources.local.json $B/experiments/figure-text-translation/ && git -C $B status --porcelain && echo CLEAN-BASE
( export TMPDIR=$T/tmp/p5-npm-base; mkdir -p $TMPDIR; cd $B && npm test > $M/npm-base.log 2>&1; echo "EXIT=$? $(date +%T)" > $M/npm-base.exit )
( export TMPDIR=$T/tmp/p5-npm-tip; mkdir -p $TMPDIR; npm test > $M/npm-tip.log 2>&1; echo "EXIT=$? $(date +%T)" > $M/npm-tip.exit )
```

### Task 12: the skeptic's four findings — a repeated config key, an invisible value line, the stale outputs' order, and the held route's rotation and measuring run

**Starts from Task 11's tree (`c85b61cc7`; Task 11 commits nothing).** The replicate verifier's skeptic found
one major and three minor gaps in Tasks 1–11. For each it applied a fix, measured it green and restored the
tree; its findings and fixes are in `~/.cache/namsbokasafn-audit/2026-10-03-step2/wf5/skeptic-findings.json`.
This task closes all four, test-first, as four commits on the local branch `scratch/c140s2-p5` (executed
2026-10-03): `0471d5448` (finding 1), `6ca0d8439` (finding 2), `d4f18c9b1` (finding 3) and `af98d00c7`
(finding 4). Every code block below was generated by a script from those commits' blobs and checked
byte-identical. Every "Replace" anchor was checked with `str.count`: it occurs exactly once at its point of
application, and exactly once in `c85b61cc7`'s blob of the file. The two exceptions are marked; they are in
Step 12 and hold text that Step 1 adds. Each expected output is what that run printed. The `free -h` lines
are left out, absolute paths are shown as `<repo>` or `$TMPDIR/tmp<random>`, vitest's per-test timings are
dropped, and the suites' long lines are cut at 230 characters with ` …`; the mutation scripts' lines
(Steps 5 and 17) and Step 21's A/B lines are shown whole, its `AB-CONFIGS` path included. The gitignored `out/` exists from Task 11 Step 1,
so `test_figure_compose.py` prints section 7's `7a` and `7b`; see Task 6 Step 2.

- Consumes:
  - from Task 6: `load_held`, `compose()`, `DERIVED_OUTPUTS`, and test_figure_compose's helpers
    (`run_prepare`, `run_wrapper`, `write_tr`, `write_config`, `load_json`, `refused`, `err_of`, `held_of`,
    `clean`, `not_spawned`, `TR12`, `K_VERBATIM` at block 3);
  - from Task 2: `heldvalues.parse_value` and `HeldValueError`;
  - from Task 8: `validateFigureConfig` and the test's `heldCfg`, `heldCorpus`, `heldOf`; `FIGURE_TEXT_CONFIG`
    (`tools/lib/figure-text-config.js`);
  - from Task 4: `heldplan.plan_block` and test_heldplan's `plan`, `run`, `PF`, `bold_of`;
  - from Task 5: `draw_held`, and test_compose_held's `plant`, `compose`, `elements`, `text_adv`,
    `fixture_adv`, `run`, `NOFLAG`, `mfonts`;
  - from Task 11, unchanged, in `~/.cache/namsbokasafn-audit/2026-10-03-step2/p5-measure/`: `py_suites.sh`,
    `make_ab_configs.py`, `validate_scratch.mjs`, `ab_run.sh`, `ab_compare.py`, and its A/B outputs
    `ab/{values,empty,base}/`.
- Produces:
  - `load_held(...)` raises, before anything is spawned, `ComposeError("the policy config <path> repeats the
    key(s) [<sorted keys>] - JSON keeps only the last of a repeated key, ...")` with `keys` empty. It fires for
    a key repeated in ANY object of the config, compared after decoding (so a JSON escape collides), and is
    checked after the parse and before `heldvalues.load_table`.
  - `compose()`'s order: `validate_inputs` → blocks.json read once → unlink `DERIVED_OUTPUTS` → `load_held` →
    write `held-values.json` → spawn → `read_report` → `verify`.
  - `repeatedKeyProblems(text) -> string[]`, exported from `tools/lib/figure-config-validate.js`. It throws on
    invalid JSON (it calls `JSON.parse` first). Otherwise it returns one problem per (object, repeated key), in
    document order: `<where> repeats the key <key> — JSON keeps only the last of a repeated key, so the earlier
    one is dropped silently; merge them into one`. `<where>` is `the config` at the root, dotted object keys
    below it, and `[n]` for an array element.
  - `heldvalues.INVISIBLE_CATEGORIES = ('Cf', 'Mn', 'Me', 'Cc')`. After the `edge-space` check, `parse_value`
    raises `HeldValueError('invisible-line', "<value!r>: line <i> has no visible character - only format,
    combining or control characters, which draw nothing")` for a line whose every character is in them. The
    validator's mirror problem is `heldBlockValues.<b>[<k>] has a line with no visible character — only
    format, combining or control characters, which draw nothing`.

**Decisions and deviations, each measured:**
1. **A repeated key refuses at ANY depth, and that deliberately differs from F9's CONTROL.** F9's control is
   about a malformed ENTRY for another figure, which `for_figure` never reads. A repeated key makes the file
   one JSON cannot read faithfully. That is the class an unparsable config already belongs to, and it refuses
   every figure wherever its syntax error sits. Two F12 arms pin the choice: a repeat under another figure, and
   a repeat in another table. The committed config has 9 objects and no repeated key, so this refuses nothing
   today.
2. **The config is parsed ONCE, with the hook**, inside the existing `try`; the skeptic's fix parsed it a second
   time. `dict(pairs)` keeps json's own last-wins result, so `config` is unchanged.
3. **The JS validator ALSO refuses a repeat, in CI.** `JSON.parse` cannot see one, so `repeatedKeyProblems`
   scans the raw text. It is sound because it runs only on text `JSON.parse` accepted, and it decodes each key
   with `JSON.parse` of the key's own slice. Three golden-copy mutants each fail a named test (Step 5): keys
   compared undecoded, escapes not skipped, and one seen-set for the whole text. The committed-config describe
   runs it on the raw file, with a positive control planted into a copy of the real text.
   `validateFigureConfig`'s signature is unchanged.
4. **`invisible-line` covers Cf, Mn, Me and Cc.** Measured against the pinned faces: all four carry the same
   2,327 code points, among them 17 Cf, 187 Mn, 2 Me and no Cc. The invisible LETTERS U+3164, U+115F, U+1160
   and U+FFA0, and U+2800, are not in the cmap, so compose already refuses them `no-glyph`. Python 3.14's
   `unicodedata` is Unicode 16.0 and Node 22's is 17.0. The two implementations can therefore disagree on a
   code point assigned in 17.0. Both places document this; nothing works around it. U+FEFF is refused by both, by
   different rules: JS `trim()` strips it (the edge-whitespace problem), Python's `strip()` does not
   (`invisible-line`).
5. **CH2g lives in CH2**, the arm whose result it reads. CH14 is the new end-to-end arm for CR1 and CR2. Its
   changed lines draw `QZtrill`, which is 23.994 pt regular and 26.499 pt bold at 9 pt. QZX and QZQ have the
   same Liberation width in both weights, which is why no existing arm could see the measuring run.
6. **Two sentences of Task 6 no longer describe the code, and Tasks 1–11 stay byte-faithful, so they are amended
   here rather than edited.** Task 6's Deviation 7 says `load_held` runs BEFORE the unlink loop; after Step 14 it
   runs after it. Task 6 Step 8 records M17 as an equivalent survivor; with the unlink loop before the pre-flight,
   F13 kills it (Step 17).
7. **Not fixed here, measured in Step 17: a `validate_inputs` refusal in a reused `--out` still leaves the
   previous run's `compose-report.json` and `held-values.json`.** The unlink loop runs after
   `validate_inputs`, as the skeptic's fix specifies. For compose-report.json this shape predates Part 5.

- [ ] **Step 1: Finding 1 (major), the failing tests. `test_figure_compose.py` F12 and the validator's `repeatedKeyProblems`**

A repeated key in `figure-text.config.json` (a figure's `heldBlockValues` entry written twice, or a block key
repeated inside one) drops the first value silently. `json.loads` and `JSON.parse` both keep only the LAST of
two equal keys. The configs in F12 are RAW TEXT, because a Python dict cannot hold a repeated key. Each arm's
PRECONDITION shows what `json.loads` keeps, which is what the old wrapper composes, with exit 0.

```python
              f'exit {prep.returncode}: {prep.stderr.strip()[-400:]}')
        tr = write_tr(Path(td) / 'tr12.json', TR12)
        me, k = json.dumps('CNX_Fixture_F12'), json.dumps(K_VERBATIM)
        # (label, the repeated key the refusal must name, the raw config, what json.loads keeps for this figure)
        arms = (
            ("this figure's entry written twice", 'CNX_Fixture_F12',
             '{"heldBlockValues": {%s: {%s: "QZ"}, %s: {%s: "QZY"}}}' % (me, k, me, k), {K_VERBATIM: 'QZY'}),
            ('a block key repeated inside its entry', K_VERBATIM,
             '{"heldBlockValues": {%s: {%s: "QZ", %s: "QZY"}}}' % (me, k, k), {K_VERBATIM: 'QZY'}),
            ('one block key spelt twice (a JSON escape decodes to the same key)', K_VERBATIM,
             '{"heldBlockValues": {%s: {"H2O (g)": "QZ", "H2O\\u0020(g)": "QZY"}}}' % me, {K_VERBATIM: 'QZY'}),
            ("a key repeated in ANOTHER figure's entry", 'No',
             '{"heldBlockValues": {%s: {%s: "QZ"}, "CNX_Other_Figure": {"No": "QZA", "No": "QZB"}}}'
             % (me, k), {K_VERBATIM: 'QZ'}),
            ('a key repeated in another table', 'CNX_Other_Figure',
             '{"retiredFigures": {"CNX_Other_Figure": "QZ one", "CNX_Other_Figure": "QZ two"}, '
             '"heldBlockValues": {%s: {%s: "QZ"}}}' % (me, k), {K_VERBATIM: 'QZ'}),
        )
        for i, (label, key, text, kept) in enumerate(arms):
            check(f'F12 PRECONDITION {label}: json.loads reads it with no error and keeps this figure '
                  f'{kept!r} - the repeat collapses silently',
                  json.loads(text)['heldBlockValues'].get('CNX_Fixture_F12') == kept, text)
            cfg = Path(td) / f'c12-{i}.json'
            cfg.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
            clean(out)
            r = run_wrapper('--out', out, '--translations', tr, '--config', cfg)
            d = load_json(out / 'compose.json') or {}
            quiet, seen = not_spawned(out)
            check(f'F12 {label}: refused before the spawn, naming the repeated key {key!r}',
                  refused(r, 1) and 'repeats the key' in err_of(d) and repr(key) in err_of(d) and quiet,
                  f'exit {r.returncode}: {d!r} {seen}')

        census = {'objects': 0, 'repeated': []}

        def hook(pairs):
            census['objects'] += 1
            ks = [p[0] for p in pairs]
            census['repeated'] += sorted({x for x in ks if ks.count(x) > 1})
            return dict(pairs)

        json.loads((HERE / 'figure-text.config.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=hook)
        check('F12 CONTROL the committed config repeats no key at any depth (an independent census, '
              'which saw its objects)', census['repeated'] == [] and census['objects'] > 1, repr(census))
        clean(out)
        cfg = Path(td) / 'c12-once.json'
        cfg.write_text('{"heldBlockValues": {%s: {%s: "QZY"}}}' % (me, k), encoding='utf-8')
        r = run_wrapper('--out', out, '--translations', tr, '--config', cfg)
        d = load_json(out / 'compose.json') or {}
        check('F12 CONTROL the same value written ONCE composes and draws it', r.returncode == 0
              and held_of(d) == [(K_VERBATIM, 3, [0])], f'exit {r.returncode}: {d!r} :: {r.stderr.strip()[-300:]}')


if _mod is not None:
```

```js
// §C140 ㊾ D5(a), a skeptic's finding (2026-10-03). JSON.parse keeps only the LAST of two equal keys,

      ['t repeats the key k', 'u repeats the key x'],

    expect(validateFigureConfig(JSON.parse(text), heldCorpus())).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refuses text that is not valid JSON, instead of scanning it', () => {
    expect(() => repeatedKeyProblems('{"a": 1,')).toThrow(SyntaxError);
  });
});
```

with:

```python
            f'the publisher checks; run figure-prepare.py for this figure again')
    # A REPEATED KEY IS REFUSED, NEVER COLLAPSED (a skeptic's finding, 2026-10-03). json.loads - and
    # JSON.parse in the validator - keeps only the LAST of two equal keys, so a figure's entry written
    # twice (say one per value-sheet row), or a block key repeated inside one, silently drops the first
    # value: that key stays send:false, lands in `missing` where verify expects it, and ships in English
    # with exit 0. The hook sees every object's keys AFTER decoding, so two spellings of one key (a JSON
    # escape) collide as JSON itself collides them; `dict(pairs)` keeps json's own last-wins result.
    # ANY depth, deliberately: like a syntax error, a repeat anywhere is a file JSON cannot read
    # faithfully, so it refuses every figure - unlike a malformed entry for ANOTHER figure, which
    # for_figure never reads. Measured on the committed config: 9 objects, no key repeated.
    repeated = []

    def _note_repeats(pairs):
        seen = set()
        for k, _ in pairs:
            if k in seen:
                repeated.append(k)
            seen.add(k)
        return dict(pairs)

    try:
        config = json.loads(Path(config_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_note_repeats)
    except Exception as exc:                      # noqa: BLE001 - reported, not raised
```

2. Replace (at c85b61cc7's line 321):

```js
/**
 * A held figure's bought block keys: `Object.keys(sidecar.blocks)`, read STRICTLY. An absent
```

with:

```js
/**
 * Every key a JSON text repeats inside one object, at any depth (§C140 ㊾ D5(a); a skeptic's finding,
 * 2026-10-03). JSON.parse keeps only the LAST of two equal keys, so the parsed config cannot show a
 * repeat: a figure's heldBlockValues entry written twice (say one per value-sheet row), or a block key
 * repeated inside one, would drop the first value with no error, and `validateFigureConfig` would pass
 * what is left. figure-compose.py refuses the same thing at compose time, with an `object_pairs_hook`;
 * this is the CI half. Each key is compared as JSON.parse DECODES it (the slice is handed to
 * JSON.parse), so two spellings of one key - `"No"` and `"N\u006f"` - collide as they collapse. A key
 * written n times is named once.
 *
 * The text is parsed first, so invalid JSON throws and the scanner below only ever reads valid JSON.
 *
 * @param {string} text  the raw config file
 * @returns {string[]} problems, in document order; empty when no key repeats
 */
export function repeatedKeyProblems(text) {
  JSON.parse(text);
  const problems = [];
  let i = 0;
  const skipSpace = () => {
    while (i < text.length && ' \t\n\r'.includes(text[i])) i += 1;
  };
  const readString = () => {
    const start = i;
    i += 1; // the opening quote
    // A backslash skips the character it escapes (the hex digits of \uXXXX are never a quote).
    while (text[i] !== '"') i += text[i] === '\\' ? 2 : 1;
    i += 1; // the closing quote
    return JSON.parse(text.slice(start, i));
  };
  const readValue = (where) => {
    skipSpace();
    const c = text[i];
    if (c === '{' || c === '[') {
      const close = c === '{' ? '}' : ']';
      const seen = new Set();
      const named = new Set();
      i += 1;
      skipSpace();
      if (text[i] === close) {
        i += 1;
        return;
      }
      for (let n = 0; ; n += 1) {
        let child = `${where}[${n}]`;
        if (c === '{') {
          skipSpace();
          const key = readString();
          if (seen.has(key) && !named.has(key)) {
            named.add(key);
            problems.push(
              `${where === '' ? 'the config' : where} repeats the key ${key} — JSON keeps only the last ` +
                'of a repeated key, so the earlier one is dropped silently; merge them into one'
            );
          }
          seen.add(key);
          child = where === '' ? key : `${where}.${key}`;
          skipSpace();
          i += 1; // the colon
        }
        readValue(child);
        skipSpace();
        const sep = text[i];
        i += 1; // a comma, or the closing bracket
        if (sep === close) return;
      }
    }
    if (c === '"') {
      readString();
      return;
    }
    while (i < text.length && !',]} \t\n\r'.includes(text[i])) i += 1; // a number, true, false, null
  };
  readValue('');
  return problems;
}

/**
 * A held figure's bought block keys: `Object.keys(sidecar.blocks)`, read STRICTLY. An absent
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Task 12 mutation probes on repeatedKeyProblems (figure-config-validate.js). Golden copy first; restore
from it and cmp after EVERY probe (never git checkout/stash). Usage: mutate_js.py <WT> <golden> J1 J2 ..."""
import filecmp
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WT, GOLD = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
TARGET = WT / 'tools' / 'lib' / 'figure-config-validate.js'
LOGS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'logs'
LOGS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
M = {
    'J1 keys compared undecoded': [("    return JSON.parse(text.slice(start, i));\n",
                                    "    return text.slice(start + 1, i - 1);\n")],
    'J2 escapes not skipped': [("    while (text[i] !== '\"') i += text[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1;\n",
                                "    while (text[i] !== '\"') i += 1;\n")],
    'J3 one seen set for the whole text': [("      const seen = new Set();\n", ""),
                                           ("  let i = 0;\n", "  let i = 0;\n  const seen = new Set();\n")],
}


def restore():
    shutil.copyfile(GOLD, TARGET)
    assert filecmp.cmp(GOLD, TARGET, shallow=False), 'RESTORE FAILED'


for mid in sys.argv[3:]:
    name = next(k for k in M if k.split()[0] == mid)
    s = GOLD.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    for a, b in M[name]:
        assert s.count(a) == 1, (name, s.count(a), a)
        s = s.replace(a, b)
    TARGET.write_text(s, encoding='utf-8')
    log = LOGS / f'mut-{mid}.log'
    try:
        with open(log, 'w') as fh:
            r = subprocess.run(['npx', 'vitest', 'run', 'tools/__tests__/figure-config-validate.test.js'],
                               cwd=str(WT), stdout=fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=400)
        rc = r.returncode
    finally:
        restore()
    fails = sorted({ln.split(' > ')[-1].strip()[:100] for ln in log.read_text(errors='replace').splitlines()
                    if ln.startswith(' FAIL  ')})
    print(f"{mid} [{name}] EXIT={rc} {'KILLED' if rc else 'SURVIVED'} fails={len(fails)}: {fails}", flush=True)
    print(f'  RESTORED cmp={filecmp.cmp(GOLD, TARGET, shallow=False)}', flush=True)
```

J1 dies only on the escape arm, which is what makes the scanner compare keys the way JSON.parse collapses them. J2 also fails on the committed config: one of its strings holds an escaped quote, so a scanner that does not skip escapes loses its place in the real file. git add experiments/figure-text-translation/figure-compose.py experiments/figure-text-translation/test_figure_compose.py tools/lib/figure-config-validate.js tools/__tests__/figure-config-validate.test.js
```bash
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
fix(figures): §C140 ㊾ D5(a) — a repeated key in the figure config is refused, at compose time and in CI, never collapsed

A skeptic's finding: json.loads and JSON.parse keep only the LAST of two equal keys, so a figure's
heldBlockValues entry written twice, or a block key repeated inside one, dropped the first value with no
error. That label stayed send:false, landed in `missing` where verify expects it, and shipped in English
with exit 0, while the validator, the pre-flight and verify all passed it.

figure-compose.py's load_held parses the config once with an object_pairs_hook that records every key an
object repeats (after decoding, so two spellings of one key collide as they collapse) and refuses before
anything is spawned, naming the keys. It refuses a repeat at ANY depth, like a syntax error: a file JSON
cannot read faithfully refuses every figure, unlike a malformed entry for another figure, which
for_figure never reads. test_figure_compose.py F12 pins five arms (entry twice, block key twice, an
escape, another figure's entry, another table), each red on the old wrapper, with two controls: an
independent census of the committed config (9 objects, no repeat) and the same value written once.

The CI half: figure-config-validate.js exports repeatedKeyProblems(text), a scanner over the RAW text
(JSON.parse first, so it only reads valid JSON; each key decoded by JSON.parse), and the committed-config
test runs it, with a positive control planted into a copy of the real text.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Finding 2, the failing tests. HV8 and three validator rows**

U+200B, U+00AD and U+034F pass every value check and are in the pinned cmap, so a value line made only of them
reaches compose, passes `no-glyph`, fits, and draws NOTHING under a `held` note. The tests write them as
escapes, never as the characters, because they are invisible in an editor.

```python
SCRIPT_BLOCK = (0x2070, 0x209F)
# The general categories that draw NOTHING on their own: format (U+200B, U+00AD, ...), non-spacing and
# enclosing combining marks (U+034F, U+0301, ...) and controls. A value line made only of these refuses
# `invisible-line` (a skeptic's finding, 2026-10-03): the pinned faces' cmap CARRIES 17 Cf, 187 Mn and
# 2 Me code points (2,327 in all, the same set in all four faces), so compose's no-glyph check passes
# them, the line fits, and the label would be erased under a `held` note. Invisible LETTERS (U+3164,
# U+115F) and U+2800 are not in the cmap, so compose refuses those `no-glyph`. ⚠️ figure-config-validate.js
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Finding 3, the failing test. `test_figure_compose.py` F13**

`compose()` ran `load_held` before the `DERIVED_OUTPUTS` unlink loop. A pre-flight refusal in a reused `--out`
therefore left the previous run's `compose-report.json` and `held-values.json` in place. F13's PRECONDITION is
a successful run that leaves all three files, so their absence afterwards is the refusal's doing.

```python
# F13 (a skeptic's finding, 2026-10-03): a pre-flight refusal in a REUSED --out. DERIVED_OUTPUTS' contract
# is that their presence afterwards MEANS this run produced them, but the pre-flight used to run BEFORE the
# unlink loop: measured, a refusal left the previous run's compose-report.json and held-values.json in
# place, and a later direct `compose.py --held-values <out>/held-values.json` would draw the PREVIOUS
# config's values (same basename, so its cross-check passes). The PRECONDITION is the successful run
# that leaves all three files, so their absence afterwards is this refusal's doing.
def f13():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        out = Path(td) / 'fig-f13'
        prep = run_prepare(FIXTURE, out, 'CNX_Fixture_F13')
        tr = write_tr(Path(td) / 'tr13.json', TR12)
        names = ('compose-report.json', 'held-values.json', 'translated.svg')
        r = run_wrapper('--out', out, '--translations', tr, '--config',
                        write_config(Path(td) / 'c13-ok.json', {'CNX_Fixture_F13': {K_VERBATIM: 'QZ'}}))
        before = {n: (out / n).exists() for n in names}
        check('F13 PRECONDITION a successful run leaves compose-report.json, held-values.json and '
              'translated.svg in the directory', prep.returncode == 0 and r.returncode == 0
              and all(before.values()), f'prepare {prep.returncode}, compose {r.returncode}: {before}')
        r = run_wrapper('--out', out, '--translations', tr, '--config',
                        write_config(Path(td) / 'c13-stale.json', {'CNX_Fixture_F13': {'QZ no such label': 'QZX'}}))
        d = load_json(out / 'compose.json') or {}
        left = {n: (out / n).exists() for n in names}
        check("F13 a pre-flight refusal in that REUSED --out leaves none of the previous run's outputs: no "
              "compose-report.json, held-values.json or translated.svg", refused(r, 1)
              and 'matches no block' in err_of(d) and not any(left.values()), f'exit {r.returncode}: {left}')


if _mod is not None:
    for _label, _fn in (('F1-F3', f1_f3), ('F4/F10', f4_f10), ('F5/F11', f5_f11), ('F6', f6),
                        ('F7', f7), ('F8', f8), ('F9', f9), ('F12', f12), ('F13', f13)):
        attempt(_label, _fn)
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Task 12 mutation probes on figure-compose.py. Golden copy first; restore from it and cmp after EVERY
probe (never git checkout/stash). Usage: python3 mutate_fc.py <WT> <golden> M17 R1 R2 U1"""
import filecmp
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WT, GOLD = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
EXP = WT / 'experiments' / 'figure-text-translation'
TARGET = EXP / 'figure-compose.py'
LOGS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'logs'

M = {
    # Task 6's M17, re-run: equivalent under Task 6's order, killed by F13 under Task 12's.
    'M17 held-values.json not unlinked': [(
        "DERIVED_OUTPUTS = ('compose.json', 'compose-report.json', 'translated.svg', 'held-values.json')",
        "DERIVED_OUTPUTS = ('compose.json', 'compose-report.json', 'translated.svg')")],
    'R1 a repeat is not refused': [("    if repeated:\n        raise ComposeError(",
                                    "    if False:\n        raise ComposeError(")],
    'R2 only a repeated heldBlockValues table is refused': [("            if k in seen:\n                repeated.append(k)\n",
                                                  "            if k in seen and k == 'heldBlockValues':\n"
                                                  "                repeated.append(k)\n")],
    'U1 the unlink back after the pre-flight': [(
        "    for name in DERIVED_OUTPUTS:\n        (out_dir / name).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n"
        "    basename, held = load_held(out_dir, blocks, tr, config_path)\n",
        "    basename, held = load_held(out_dir, blocks, tr, config_path)\n"
        "    for name in DERIVED_OUTPUTS:\n        (out_dir / name).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n")],
}


def restore():
    shutil.copyfile(GOLD, TARGET)
    assert filecmp.cmp(GOLD, TARGET, shallow=False), 'RESTORE FAILED'


env = dict(os.environ, FIGTEXT_PYLIBS='./pylibs')
for mid in sys.argv[3:]:
    name = next(k for k in M if k.split()[0] == mid)
    s = GOLD.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    for a, b in M[name]:
        assert s.count(a) == 1, (name, s.count(a), a)
        s = s.replace(a, b)
    TARGET.write_text(s, encoding='utf-8')
    log = LOGS / f'mut-{mid}.log'
    try:
        with open(log, 'w') as fh:
            r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'test_figure_compose.py'], cwd=str(EXP), env=env,
                               stdout=fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=400)
        rc = r.returncode
    finally:
        restore()
    fails = [ln[8:].split(':')[0][:100] for ln in log.read_text(errors='replace').splitlines()
             if ln.startswith('  FAIL  ')]
    print(f"{mid} [{name}] EXIT={rc} {'KILLED' if rc else 'SURVIVED'} fails={len(fails)}: {fails}", flush=True)
    print(f'  RESTORED cmp={filecmp.cmp(GOLD, TARGET, shallow=False)}', flush=True)
```

```python
weight) and a fake has_glyph. HP16 adds the only non-pure inputs: a test-local cairo width (hint
metrics off - compose.lin_advance's measure, copied) and the real figis cmap, against each block's REAL
committed container. No file IO but one read of evidence/2026-10-03-c140-held/held-geometry.json;
nothing is spawned, nothing is drawn.

```

3. Replace (at d4f18c9b1's line 715):

```python

attempt('HP16-pins', hp16_pins)
```

with:

```python

# ── HP18 (skeptic, 2026-10-03) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Every value-sheet line is rot 0 and every changed line shares its weight with the block's first run, so
# neither the ALONG/PROJ axes nor the per-line measuring run was pinned: a planner reading x/y, or measuring
# with block[0], passed HP0-HP17. Both are planted here, against a width that depends on the weight of the
# run it is handed, as compose's seg_width does.
def hp18():
    def wdep(chars, size, r):
        k = 0.62 if bold_of(PF)(r) else 0.5
        return sum(k * size * (st.ratio if st is not None else 1.0) for _, st in chars)

    r90 = [dict(run('QQ', 9.0, 100.0, 20.0), rot=90.0)]
    a, e = FT.along(r90[0]), FT.along(r90[0]) + r90[0]['adv']
    lay = plan(r90, 'QZQZ', PF, width=wdep).lines[0][1]
    want = (a + e) / 2 - wdep([(c, None) for c in 'QZQZ'], 9.0, r90[0]) / 2
    check('HP18a a rot-90 changed line is centred on its own ALONG extent, at its own PROJ',
          abs(lay['x0'][0] - want) < 1e-6 and abs(lay['top'] - FT.proj(r90[0])) < 1e-6,
          f"x0 {lay['x0']} want {want}; top {lay['top']} proj {FT.proj(r90[0])}")

    mixed = [run('Ab', 9.0, 10.0, 50.0), run('Cd', 9.0, 10.0, 39.0, font='T/B')]
    p = plan(mixed, 'Ab\nQZQZQZ', PF, width=wdep)
    want = (10.0 + 19.0) / 2 - wdep([(c, None) for c in 'QZQZQZ'], 9.0, mixed[1]) / 2
    check("HP18b a changed BOLD line under a regular first line is measured in ITS OWN run's weight",
          [x[0] for x in p.lines] == ['runs', 'layout'] and abs(p.lines[1][1]['x0'][0] - want) < 1e-6,
          f"{[x[0] for x in p.lines]} x0 {p.lines[-1][1].get('x0') if p.lines[-1][0] == 'layout' else None} want {want}")


attempt('HP18', hp18)
attempt('HP16-pins', hp16_pins)
```

`$T/mut/held/mutate4.py` holds the skeptic's six mutants (CR1, CR2, CR6 in compose.py; W1, R1, R2 in heldplan.py):
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Task 12 (finding 4) mutation probes: the held route's rotation and per-line measuring run. Golden copy of
the target first; restore from it and cmp after EVERY probe (never git checkout/stash).
Usage: python3 mutate4.py <WT> <golden> <suite.py> CR1 CR2 ...   (the target is named by the mutant)"""
import filecmp
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WT, GOLD, SUITE = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
EXP = WT / 'experiments' / 'figure-text-translation'
LOGS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'logs'

M = {
    # compose.py: draw_held's lambdas and its offset bookkeeping (the skeptic's CR1, CR2, CR6)
    'CR1 draw_held draws the layout at rot 0': ('compose.py', [(
        "            draw_layout(layout, lambda j, r0=vl[0]: r0, rot)\n",
        "            draw_layout(layout, lambda j, r0=vl[0]: r0, 0)\n")]),
    'CR2 the width measured with the block\'s first run': ('compose.py', [(
        "                                   width=lambda chars, size, run: seg_width(chars, run, size),\n",
        "                                   width=lambda chars, size, run: seg_width(chars, b[0], size),\n")]),
    'CR6 a layout entry does not advance the offset': ('compose.py', [(
        "            laid.append(layout)\n            off += len(vl)\n",
        "            laid.append(layout)\n")]),
    # heldplan.py: the axes and the measuring run (the skeptic's W1, R1, R2)
    'W1 decide measures with block[0]': ('heldplan.py', [(
        "lambda chars, size, j: width(chars, size, run0), box, cues)",
        "lambda chars, size, j: width(chars, size, block[0]), box, cues)")]),
    'R1 the cues read x, not ALONG': ('heldplan.py', [(
        "                    starts=[min(FT.along(r) for r in vl)],\n"
        "                    ends=[max(FT.along(r) + r['adv'] for r in vl)],\n",
        "                    starts=[min(r['x'] for r in vl)],\n"
        "                    ends=[max(r['x'] + r['adv'] for r in vl)],\n")]),
    'R2 proj reads y': ('heldplan.py', [(
        "                    projs=[FT.proj(run0)])", "                    projs=[run0['y']])")]),
}

env = dict(os.environ, FIGTEXT_PYLIBS='./pylibs')
for mid in sys.argv[4:]:
    name = next(k for k in M if k.split()[0] == mid)
    target, edits = M[name]
    target = EXP / target
    assert GOLD.name.startswith(target.name), (GOLD, target)
    s = GOLD.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    for a, b in edits:
        assert s.count(a) == 1, (name, s.count(a), a)
        s = s.replace(a, b)
    target.write_text(s, encoding='utf-8')
    log = LOGS / f'mut-{mid}-{SUITE}.log'
    try:
        with open(log, 'w') as fh:
            r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', SUITE], cwd=str(EXP), env=env,
                               stdout=fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=500)
        rc = r.returncode
    finally:
        shutil.copyfile(GOLD, target)
        assert filecmp.cmp(GOLD, target, shallow=False), 'RESTORE FAILED'
    fails = [ln[8:].split(':')[0][:110] for ln in log.read_text(errors='replace').splitlines()
             if ln.startswith('  FAIL  ')]
    print(f"{mid} [{name}] vs {SUITE} EXIT={rc} {'KILLED' if rc else 'SURVIVED'} fails={len(fails)}: {fails}",
          flush=True)
    print(f'  RESTORED cmp={filecmp.cmp(GOLD, target, shallow=False)}', flush=True)
```

```bash
test(figures): §C140 ㊾ D5(a) — the held route's rotation and per-line measuring run are pinned, in the planner and end to end

A skeptic's finding: every value-sheet line is rot 0 and every changed line shares its weight with its
block's first run, so neither the ALONG/PROJ axes nor the run a changed line is measured with was
pinned. Six non-equivalent mutants passed all three held suites: in heldplan.py the cues reading x (R1),
the proj reading y (R2) and decide measuring with block[0] (W1); in compose.py draw_held drawing the
layout at rot 0 (CR1), its width lambda measuring with b[0] (CR2), and a 'layout' entry not advancing
the source offset (CR6, which compares OxStNonmts' line 2 with `To` and names the block `localized`).

test_heldplan.py HP18 (the skeptic's code) plants a rot-90 line and a bold changed line under a regular
first line, against a width that depends on the run's weight. test_compose_held.py gains the RM plant
and CH14: a rot-90 held line drawn rotated, its pen at the run's own x and centred on its own extent; a
bold changed line drawn bold and centred on its own source line, measured with the sentinel QZtrill,
which is 2.5 pt wider in bold (QZX, QZQ are the same width in both weights, which is why no arm could
see the measuring run). CH2g asserts OxStNonmts' `localized` equals the no-flag run's ([]). Measured
with golden-copy mutants, restored and cmp'd after every round: all six SURVIVE c85b61cc7's suites;
here W1/R1/R2 fail HP18 and CH14, CR1 and CR2 fail CH14, CR6 fails CH2g. heldplan.py's docstring now
names HP0-HP18.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
```
